Need measure M_w matvec and solve. Let's derive efficient matvec: y_i = ∑_{k=0}^{n-1} x_k w_{i+k}. This is cross-correlation of x and w. Code:
```
def hankel_matvec(x, w):
    n=x.size
    m=w.size # 2n-1
    M=n+m-1
    N=next_pow2(M)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x[::-1], N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(X*W,N)
    return full[n-1: n-1+n].copy()
```

def multiplicative_update(a, h, eta, scale=None):
    if scale is None: hmax=max(abs? h); if hmax<=... return a
    loga = log(a) - eta*h/hmax - max(loga)
    new = exp(loga)
    # optional floor? zeros okay
    return normalize(new)
```

```
def make_active_w(d, M, gap_frac, top_k, max_count, power):
    gap = M*gap_frac
    if gap <=0: but use min_gap= (M - min(d))*0.01?
    wraw = np.power(np.maximum(d - (M - gap),0), power)
    wsum=wraw.sum()
    if wsum <=0 or count<top_k: take top top_k uniform
    elif count > max_count: take top max_count weighted by power maybe or uniform
    normalize.
```

Let's more rigorously compute `corr_pair`/`hankel_matvec`. For weights w over conv length L=2n-1. The output h length n: h_m = ∑_{k=0}^{n-1} a_k w_{m+k}. Does this equal H_w a? H_{m,k}=w_{m+k}; yes. Need orientation: conv C_j with j=m+k? C_j = ∑_k a_k a_{j-k}. H_j a_m = a_{j-m}? Weighted H_w a_m = ∑_j w_j (H_j a)_m =∑_j w_j a_{j-m}. Let k=j-m => j=m+k => ∑_k a_k w_{m+k}. Good. So h_m=corr pair. `normalize_positive(a)`:
```
def normalize_positive(a, floor=1e-300):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
   if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   a = a + floor
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 1e-12: return np.full(a.size, floor?) no, uniform
   return a/s
```
But adding floor to all entries changes sum if a has exact zeros; negligible. For final, don't add floor? Final can have zeros. `normalize_positive` for internal. If the floor sum for n large =4e-26, negligible. Use tiny floor 1e-300.

`active_weights(d, M, gap_frac, top_k, max_count, power)`:
```
def active_weights(d, M, gap_frac, top_k, max_count, power):
   L=d.size
   gap=M*gap_frac
   if gap <= 1e-300:
       # top top_k uniform
   w=np.zeros(L)
   thresh=M-gap
   mask=d >= thresh
   # if too few top
   cnt=mask.sum()
   if cnt < top_k:
       idx=np.argpartition(d, -top_k)[-top_k:]
       w[idx]=1.0
   else:
       w[mask] = np.maximum(d[mask]-thresh, 0.0)**power
       s=w.sum()
       if s < 1e-300 or cnt > max_count:
           kc=min(max_count,L)
           idx=np.argpartition(d, -kc)[-kc:]
           # maybe keep weighted among top kc:
           w2=np.zeros(L)
           w2[idx] = d[idx] - min(d[idx]) + 1e-300
           # or power; simpler uniform? Use power of d.
           # But if we use uniform, okay.
           w=w2
           s=w.sum()
       if s>0: w/=s
       else uniform.
   return w
```
If M=0, uniform. For power, if d-thresh maybe 0 for threshold exactly; use.
Maybe better for count>max: use top max weighted by power of d - min top. But H matvec cost unaffected.

`try_direction(a, base_log, h, eta_list, n, best_a, best_sc)`:
```
def try_direction(a, base_log, h, eta_list, n, best_a, best_sc):
    hmax = max(abs(float(np.max(h)), float(-np.min(h))))
    if hmax < 1e-300: return best_a,best_sc
    h_scaled = h / hmax
    for eta in eta_list:
       if eta <=0: continue
       logn = base_log - eta*h_scaled
       # Protect from overflow: logn - max(logn)
       m = float(np.max(logn))
       new = np.exp(logn - m)
       # Add floor? normalize_positive will add floor, but if exp underflows some zeros. use np.where(new>0,new,0)
       new = normalize_positive(new)
       sc = exact_score(new)
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=new
    return best_a,best_sc
```
This computes conv each eta. Could optimize if `eta` small with previous? okay. Need `exact_score` does `fast_self_conv` and sum. Since new normalized, S ~1. Good.
If improvement tiny due to numerical, use tolerance? We can accept any < -1e-12. In main, if best_sc improved > 1e-12, count. If within, no. Use `if best_sc_iter < best_sc - 1e-12`.

But `try_direction` will accept candidate with score slightly lower but maybe due to random? Use exact deterministic.

Mirror loop directions selection:
```
def mirror_descent_optimize(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", eta_base=1.0, max_iters=None):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_positive(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a); print.
    eta=eta_base
    gap=0.05
    topk=10
    stagnant=0
    it=0
    max_stagnant=60
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       if max_iters and it>=max_iters: break
       conv=fast_self_conv(a)
       M=float(np.max(conv))*n
       sc=2*M
       if sc<best_sc-1e-13: best...; stagnant=0
       base_log=np.log(np.maximum(a, Floor)); base_log -= float(np.max(base_log))
       best_iter_a=a; best_iter_sc=sc
       # LSE several betas: choose list maybe every iteration.
       # To reduce, select 3 betas based on it: [beta_low, beta_mid, beta_high]
       # Use 4: 20, 100, 1000, 5000
       betas = (20, 100, 500, 2000)
       for beta in betas:
           w=softmax_weights(d, beta)
           h=corr_pair(a,w)
           # etas: [eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*2]
           best_iter_a,best_iter_sc=try_direction(a,base_log,h, (eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*2), n, best_iter_a,best_iter_sc)
       # active: gap adapt, topk
       w=active_weights(d, M, gap, topk, max_count)
       h=corr_pair(a,w)
       etas=(eta*0.2, eta*0.5, eta, eta*1.5, eta*2.5)
       best_iter...=try_direction(...)
       # maybe second active with larger gap
       w=active_weights(d, M, min(0.3, gap*2), max(3,topk//2), max_count)
       ...
       if best_iter_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           a=best_iter_a; best_sc=best_iter_sc; best_a=a.copy(); stagnant=0; # adjust eta/gap
           if eta < 4: eta = min(4, eta*1.05)
           # maybe shrink gap slightly if top? keep
           if verbose and it%20: print
       else:
           stagnant +=1
           eta = max(0.05, eta*0.8)
           gap = min(0.3, gap*1.15) # broaden? actually if no improvement with narrow, broaden
           topk = min(100, int(topk*1.2)+1)
           if stagnant >= max_stagnant:
               break
       it+=1
    # final L-BFGS? maybe outside
    return best_sc,best_a
```

`lbfgs_softmax_fun(y,beta)`:
```
def lbfgs_softmax_fun(y,beta):
    n=y.size
    if n==0: return inf, np.array([])
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    # for fixed first, subtract y[0]
    if y.size > 1:
        y_work = y - y[0]
    else: ...
    a = np.exp(y_work - np.max(y_work)); a/=a.sum()
    conv = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = n*conv
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not finite or <=0: return large, zeros
    z = beta*(d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0: ...
    w=e/s
    lse = dmax + math.log(s)/beta
    val = 2.0*lse
    h=corr_pair(a,w)
    grad_a=4.0*n*h
    sdot=float(np.dot(a,grad_a))
    grad_y=a*(grad_a - sdot*a)
    if n>1: grad_y[0]=0.0
    grad_y=np.nan_to_num(grad_y,...)
    return float(val), grad_y
```

```
def softmax(y):
   ym = y - max(y)
   e=exp(ym)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: uniform
   return e/s
```

def optimize_level(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
    n=len(a)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=deadline-t0
    if total <= 10: return exact_score(a), a
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    # Mirror 65%
    d1 = t0 + 0.65*total
    sc,a = mirror_descent_optimize(a, d1, verbose=verbose, tag=tag+"_m1", rng=rng)
    if sc<best_sc: ...
    # L-BFGS 15%
    d2 = t0 + 0.80*total
    sc,a = lbfgs_polish(a, d2, verbose=verbose, tag=tag+"_l")
    if sc<best_sc: ...
    # Mirror final 20%
    sc,a = mirror_descent_optimize(a, deadline, verbose=verbose, tag=tag+"_m2", rng=rng, eta_base=0.5)
    if sc<best_sc: ...
    return best_sc,best_a
```

def log_ratio(a):
   a=np.asarray(a)
   pos=a>1e-300
   ref = np.median(a[pos]) if pos.any() else 1.0
   y=np.log(a/ ref)  # if a zero, -inf; clip
   y=np.clip(y, -80, 80)
   y[0]-=y[0]
   y[0]=0.0
   return y
```

try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len
except Exception:
    next_fast_len = None

TINY = 1e-300
MAXY = 80.0

def next_fft_len(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    if next_fast_len is not None:
       return int(next_fast_len(m))
    p=1
    while p<m: p <<=1
    return p
```
But if next_fast_len imported, for m=1 returns 1. For rfft with length 1 okay. For m maybe 0? protect.

`fast_self_conv(a)`:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   N=next_fft_len(2*n-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return c
```
Could set negative small to 0? For scoring, max if negative? Conv nonneg; if all negative due to error? do `c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)`? But LSE derivative might need small negative? Not. Use in scoring clipped. In LSE d, if some small negative, z negative, weight zero; okay. Max top positive. Maybe don't clip in conv for gradient? But if w broad, h may be impacted by negative noise. Clip conv to 0 in `fast_self_conv`? It might slightly bias. Use `return np.maximum(c,0.0)`? Since true nonnegative; negative numerical ~-1e-20; harmless. Do it to avoid LSE weird. But if all c are zero? okay.
However median of a? no.
Use `c[c<0 & c>-1e-15]=0; c=np.maximum(c,0)`.

`corr_pair(a,w)`:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
   a=np.asarray...; w=np.asarray...
   n=a.size; m=w.size
   if n==0 or m==0: return empty
   M=n+m-1
   N=next_fft_len(M)
   X=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
   full=np.fft.irfft(X*W,N)
   return full[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
```
Clip? h can be negative, don't.
`nan_to_num` h.

`rfft_reversed(a)`:
```
def rfft_reversed(a):
   n=a.size
   N=next_fft_len(3*n-2)
   return np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N), N
def hankel_matvec_precomp(w, X, N, n):
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   full=np.fft.irfft(X*W,N)
   return full[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
```
Potential N for w length exactly 2n-1. If active w from d length 2n-1. Good. If later use w other length? no for precomp.

`normalize_positive(a, tinfy=True)`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   a=a + 1e-300
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 1e-12:
       return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
   return a/s
```
Adding 1e-300 sum negligible. If a.size large, okay.
For final, no add tiny? Use `normalize_final`.
```
def finalize_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray...
   a=np.nan_to_num(...)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return [0.5]*1024? need sum .? 0.5*1024=512 >0.01; score maybe finite. But target. fallback uniform len1024 values 1/1024 sum1.
   a=a/s
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```
If a length 0 fallback.

`resample_sequence(a,n)`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
   a=np.asarray...
   m=a.size
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if m==int(n): return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,int(n))
   out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```
Could use `out=np.maximum(out,0)`. Good.

`softmax(y)` top-level:
```
def softmax_stable(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if y.size==0: return np.zeros(0)
   ymax=float(np.max(y))
   if not finite: ...
   e=np.exp(y-ymax- maybe if ymax large y-ymax <=0)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: uniform
   return e/s
```

`softmax_weights(d,beta)`:
```
def softmax_weights(d,beta):
   d=np.asarray(d,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   L=d.size
   if L==0: return np.zeros(0)
   d=np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   dmax=float(np.max(d))
   if dmax < 1e-300 or beta <=0:
      return np.ones(L)/L
   z=beta*(d-dmax)
   z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s <=1e-300: return np.ones(L)/L
   w=e/s
   return w
```
For LSE objective, weights should be e^{β(d-dmax)}/sum; same.
If want exact LSE val in lbfgs, compute also e sum. But for weights in mirror, okay. For L-BFGS, we need lse val; can recompute exp or call separate. To avoid duplicate, in lbfgs compute directly.

   idx=np.argpartition(d, L-k, ?)
```

`gap_weights(d,M,gap_frac,top_k,max_count,power)`:
```
def gap_weights(d,M,gap_frac,top_k,max_count,power=1.0):
   L=d.size
   if L==0: return np.zeros(0)
   if M <=1e-300: return np.ones(L)/L
   gap=M*gap_frac
   if gap <=1e-300:
       return top_weights(d, max(1, min(top_k,L)))
   thresh=M-gap
   mask=d >= thresh
   cnt=int(mask.sum())
   w=np.zeros(L)
   if cnt < top_k:
       idx=np.argpartition(d, -min(top_k,L))[-min(top_k,L):]
       w[idx]=1.0
   else:
       vals=d[mask].copy()
       # to avoid all zero if threshold exact
       maxv=float(np.max(vals))
       if maxv - thresh <= 1e-300:
           w[mask]=1.0
       else:
           w[mask]=np.power(vals - thresh, power)
       cnt=int(w.nonzero()[0].size)
       s=float(w.sum())
       if cnt > max_count or s <= 1e-300:
           kc=int(min(max(1,max_count), L))
           idx=np.argpartition(d, -kc)[-kc:]
           w=np.zeros(L)
           vals=d[idx]
           vmin=float(np.min(vals))
           if power <=1: w[idx]=vals-vmin+1e-300
           else: w[idx]=np.power(vals-vmin+1e-300, power)
           s=float(w.sum())
       if s>1e-300: w/=s
       else: w=top_weights(d, max(1,min(max_count,L)))
   return w
```
Need after if cnt>max_count, we overwrite w; okay.

`try_direction` with precomputed base_log.
```
def try_direction(a, base_log, h, etas, n, best_a, best_sc):
   h=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64)
   if h.size != n: return
   h = h - float(np.mean(h))
   scale=float(np.max(np.abs(h))) # np.max on abs; for n large okay
   if scale < 1e-16: return
   h_scaled = h / scale
   for eta in etas:
      if eta <=0 or not finite: continue
      logn=base_log - eta*h_scaled
      # If any logn large? clipped: np.clip(logn, -745, 709)? base_log max 0, h_scaled in [-1,1], eta <=8 => within [-8,8], safe. But if eta huge? cap etas at 8.
      # For numerical, subtract max before exp to avoid underflow? Not needed but can:
      lmax=float(np.max(logn))
      cand=np.exp(logn-lmax)
      # if cand sum <=0, skip
      cand = normalize_positive(cand)
      sc=exact_score(cand)
      if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=cand
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Use `np.abs(h)` may allocate; can do max of h and -min(h) to save.
```
hmaxp=float(np.max(h)); hmaxn=float(-np.min(h)); scale=max(hmaxp,hmaxn)
```
Center h: `hm=float(np.mean(h)); h_centered = h - hm` allocating. Use `h = h - hm`. Fine.

`mirror_descent_optimize` details:
```
def mirror_descent_optimize(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", rng=None):
   n=len(a)
   a=normalize_positive(a)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
   if verbose: print...
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(987654321)
   eta=1.0
   gap=0.05
   topk=20
   stagnant=0
   perturbs=0
   it=0
   max_stag=80
   # compute max_count
   max_count=max(20, int(0.02*n))
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       if it > 1000000: break
       conv=fast_self_conv(a) # maybe clip
       d=conv * n
       dmax=float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
       sc=2.0*dmax
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); stagnant=0
       else:
           # exact score should match; due to candidate maybe we already have best_sc; if input score slightly lower? handle
           # if sc > best_sc? keep best.
           pass
       # sym every 8 iterations
       if it % 16 == 0:
           sym=normalize_positive(0.5*(a + a[::-1]))
           scs=exact_score(sym)
           if scs < sc - 1e-14:
              a=sym; sc=scs
           if scs < best_sc - 1e-14:
              best_sc=scs; best_a=sym.copy()
       base_log=np.log(np.maximum(a, TINY)); base_log -= float(np.max(base_log))
       Xrev,Nh = rfft_reversed(a)
       best_a_it=best_a # but we want track iteration best to accept
       best_sc_it=sc
       # LSE betas
       betas=(50.0, 400.0, 3000.0)
       etas=(max(0.02,0.4*eta), eta, min(6.0, 2.0*eta))
       for beta in betas:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0: break
           w=softmax_weights(d,beta)
           h=hankel_matvec_precomp(w, Xrev, Nh, n)
           best_a_it,best_sc_it=try_direction(a, base_log, h, etas, n, best_a_it, best_sc_it)
       # gap active
       w=gap_weights(d,dmax,gap,max(5,topk),max_count,1.0)
       h=hankel_matvec_precomp(w,Xrev,Nh,n)
       etas_active=(max(0.02,0.3*eta), eta, min(6.0,1.8*eta))
       best_a_it,best_sc_it=try_direction(...)
       # top narrow uniform
       w=top_weights(d, max(3, topk//2))
       h=...
       best_a_it,best_sc_it=try_direction(...)
       # second gap broader if time
       if time...:
          w=gap_weights(d,dmax,min(0.3,gap*2),max(5,topk//2),max_count,2.0)
          h=...
       if best_sc_it < best_sc - 1e-13:
           a=best_a_it.copy()
           best_sc=best_sc_it
           best_a=a.copy()
           stagnant=0
           # adapt
           if eta < 4.0: eta = min(4.0, eta*1.03)
           # gap slight tighten? if top improved maybe keep; maybe if gap too broad?
           if gap > 0.02: gap = max(0.02, gap*0.98)
           # topk slight
           if topk > 8: topk = max(8, int(topk*0.97))
       else:
           stagnant += 1
           eta = max(0.05, eta*0.85)
           gap = min(0.3, gap*1.15)
           topk = min(120, int(topk*1.15)+1)
           if stagnant >= max_stag:
               if perturbs < 5:
                   a = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.05 + 0.05*perturbs)
                   # maybe after perturb set best_sc_it exact? next iter
                   perturbs +=1
                   stagnant=0
                   eta=0.8; gap=0.05; topk=20
                   # If verbose print
               else:
                   break
       it +=1
   return float(best_sc), np.asarray(best_a)
```

`optimize_level` with rng.
```
def optimize_level(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", rng=None):
   a=normalize_positive(a)
   best_a=a; best_sc=exact_score(a)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   total=deadline-t0
   if total <=15: return best_sc,best_a
   if verbose: print level start
   p1=t0+0.55*total
   sc,a=mirror_descent_optimize(a,p1,verbose,tag+"_m1",rng)
   if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
   p2=t0+0.80*total
   sc,a=lbfgs_polish(a,p2,verbose,tag+"_l",rng)
   if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
   sc,a=mirror_descent_optimize(a,deadline,verbose,tag+"_m2",rng, eta_base=0.6)
   if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
   # optional final lbfgs if time left 20? maybe skip
   if verbose print
   return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    a=normalize_positive(a)
    n=a.size
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    if strength <=0: return a
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,8))
    if mode==0:
       noise=smooth_noise(n,rng)
       q=a*np.exp(strength*0.25*noise)
    elif mode==1:
       q=a*np.exp(strength*0.5*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode==2:
       t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25))
       q=(1-t)*a + t*(np.ones(n)/n)
    elif mode==3:
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       q=a*(1.0 + strength*0.5*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*float(rng.integers(1,6))*x))
    elif mode==4:
       q=a*(1.0 + strength*0.5*(2.0*x-1.0)**2) # need x defined? define in branch
    elif mode==5:
       q=(1-t)*a + t*... v-shape? 
    else:
       q=a*rng.uniform(0.8,1.2,n)
    return normalize_positive(np.maximum(q,0.0))
```
Need x defined when branch. Could define `x=np.linspace(0,1,n)` at top per call, cost small.

def choose_best_init(n, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   candidates=[]
   candidates.append(np.ones(n))
   for alpha in (0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5): candidates.append(1+alpha*np.cos(2πx))
   candidates.append((1-x)**2+x? etc)
   for _ in range(4):
       # random smooth using FFT
       N=next_fft_len(n)
       freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N)*N
       power=1/((np.abs(freqs)+1)**float(rng.uniform(1.5,3.5)))
       power[0]=0
       phase=rng.uniform(-π,π,size=len(power))
       noise=np.fft.irfft(power*np.exp(1j*phase), N)[:n]
       noise-=noise.mean()
       candidates.append(1+0.5*noise)
   for _ in range(3): candidates.append(0.1+0.9*rng.random(n))
   best_a=normalize_positive(candidates[0]); best_sc=exact_score(best_a)
   for c in candidates[1:]:
       arr=normalize_positive(c)
       sc=exact_score(arr)
       if sc<best_sc: ...
   return best_sc,best_a
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start=time.monotonic()

        h=globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)

            p=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()

        else: n1=16384

```
def exact_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   S=float(a.sum())
   if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf
   c=fast_self_conv(a)
   if c.size==0: return np.inf
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if cmax < 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax): cmax=0.0
   return float(2.0*n*cmax/(S*S))
```

Now, `smooth_noise`: Need kernel length not > n. Use:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng):
    k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, n//200)))
    if k % 2 == 0: k +=1
    k = min(k, n if n%2==1 else n-1)
    k=max(3,k)
    c=k//2
    sig=max(1.0,c/2.0)
    kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-c)**2)/(2*sig*sig))
    kern/=kern.sum()
    noise=np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, mode='same')
    noise -= noise.mean()
    return noise
```
If n small <5, but n large. Good. `np.convolve` direct small kernel O(nk) maybe if k~200 and n 40k => 8M, okay for perturb few times. Could use FFT for noise? Direct okay.

```
def excess_weights(d, m):
   L=d.size; m=min(m,L)
   # get top m without full sort: threshold via argpartition
   if m<=0: uniform
   idx=np.argpartition(d, L-m)[-m:]
   mt=float(np.mean(d[idx]))
   excess=np.maximum(d-mt,0)
   s=excess.sum()
   if s<=...: top_weights(d,m)
   return excess/s
```

`choose_best_init` code without nested:
```
def choose_best_init(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cands=[]
    cands.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for alpha in (0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0):
       arr=1.0 + alpha*np.cos(2.0*math.pi*x)
       cands.append(arr)
    for alpha in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       cands.append(1.0 + alpha*(2*x-1)**2)
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(0.0,1-np.abs(2*x-1)), p)+0.01)
    for rate in (1.0,3.0,8.0):
       cands.append(np.exp(-rate*x)+np.exp(-rate*(1-x))+0.01)
    N=next_fft_len(n)
    for _ in range(4):
       freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N)*N
       power=1.0/((np.abs(freqs)+1.0)**float(rng.uniform(1.5,4.0)))
       power[0]=0.0
       phase=rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi, size=len(power))
       spec=power*np.exp(1j*phase)
       noise=np.fft.irfft(spec,N)[:n]
       noise-=float(noise.mean())
       cands.append(1.0 + 0.6*noise)
    for _ in range(2):
       cands.append(0.1+0.9*rng.random(n))
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for c in cands:
       arr=normalize_positive(c)
       sc=exact_score(arr)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=arr
    return best_sc, best_a
```
No closures. `np.exp(1j*phase)` okay. If n small, fine.

def log_ratio(a, fix=0):
   a=...
   fix=int(fix)%a.size
   pos=a>1e-200
   if pos.any(): logs=np.log(a[pos]); ref=float(np.exp(np.mean(logs)))

   y=np.log(a/ref)
   y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-MAXY, posinf=MAXY, neginf=-MAXY)
   y=y-y[fix]
   y=np.clip(y, -MAXY, MAXY)
   y[fix]=0.0

```

Implementation of `gap_weights` robust:
```
def gap_weights(d, M, gap_frac, top_k, max_count, power=1.0):
    L=d.size
    if L==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if M <= 1e-300: return np.ones(L)/L
    top_k=int(max(1,min(L,top_k)))
    max_count=int(max(1,min(L,max_count)))
    gap=M*float(gap_frac)
    if gap <=1e-300:
        return top_weights(d, top_k)
    thresh=M-gap
    w=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
    mask=d >= thresh
    cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
    if cnt < top_k:
        kc=top_k
        idx=np.argpartition(d, -kc)[-kc:]
        w[idx]=1.0
    else:
        vals=d[mask]
        excess=vals - thresh
        maxex=float(np.max(excess))
        if maxex <= 1e-300 or power <= 0.0:
            w[mask]=1.0
        else:
            w[mask]=np.power(excess, float(power))
        cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(w))
        s=float(w.sum())
        if cnt > max_count or s <= 1e-300:
            kc=max_count
            idx=np.argpartition(d, -kc)[-kc:]
            w=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
            vals=d[idx]
            vmin=float(np.min(vals))
            excess=vals-vmin
            maxex=float(np.max(excess))
            if maxex <=1e-300 or power <=0.0:
                w[idx]=1.0
            else:
                w[idx]=np.power(excess, float(power))
            s=float(w.sum())
    s=float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return top_weights(d, max_count)
    return w/s
```
If `cnt < top_k` and `top_k` maybe L? argpartition with -L okay? If kc==L, `np.argpartition(d,-L)` returns all? It works. But if kc==L, can return uniform. Add if kc>=L: return uniform.
In top_weights same.

def top_weights(d,k):
 L=d.size; k=int(max(1,min(k,L)))
 if k>=L: return np.ones(L)/L
 idx=np.argpartition(d, -k)[-k:]
 w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/k
 return w
```
If k=0? max 1.

`excess_weights`:
```
def excess_weights(d, m):
 L=d.size; m=int(max(1,min(m,L)))
 if m>=L: return np.ones(L)/L
 idx=np.argpartition(d, -m)[-m:]
 mt=float(np.mean(d[idx]))
 excess=np.maximum(d-mt, 0.0)
 s=float(excess.sum())
 if s <= 1e-300:
    return top_weights(d,m)
 return excess/s
```

```
def smooth_noise(n, rng):
    maxk = max(3, min(n, 41))
    k = int(rng.integers(3, maxk + 1))
    if k % 2 == 0: k += 1
    if k > n:
        k = n if n % 2 == 1 else max(1,n-1)
    if k % 2 == 0: k += 1
    if k < 1: return np.zeros(n)
    c = k // 2
    sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
    kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2 * sig * sig))
    kern /= kern.sum()
    noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, mode='same')
    noise -= noise.mean()
    return noise
```

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
    2n * max_j conv(a, a)_j / sum(a)^2.

Approach:
- Normalize the sequence to sum 1; the score is scale invariant.
- Use FFT-based self-convolution and FFT-based derivative subgradients for
  weighted active convolution lags.
- Run a multi-resolution search: improve a parent sequence at increasing
  lengths (roughly 10240 -> 20480 -> 32768 -> 43680).
- At each length, use adaptive multiplicative mirror-descent steps driven by
  LSE-smoothed and top-convolution active sets, followed by L-BFGS refinement
  of softmax-smoothed LSE objectives, then another multiplicative polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len
except Exception:
    next_fast_len = None


TINY = 1e-300
MAXY = 80.0


def next_fft_len(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if next_fast_len is not None:
        try:
            return int(next_fast_len(m))
        except Exception:
            pass
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = next_fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    For a and convolution-weight vector w of length 2n-1, return
    h_m = sum_k a_k w_{m+k}, m=0..n-1.
    This is the subgradient H_w(a) for the weighted self-convolution.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = n + m - 1
    N = next_fft_len(L)

    X = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(X * W, N)
    h = full[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()
    return np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def rfft_reversed(a):
    n = int(np.asarray(a).size)
    N = next_fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    return np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)[::-1], N), N


def hankel_matvec_precomp(w, X, N, n):
    W = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(X * W, N)
    h = full[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()
    return np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a = a + TINY
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def finalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
    a = a / s
    if a.size == 0:
        return [0.5]
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def softmax_stable(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(y.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-MAXY, posinf=MAXY, neginf=-MAXY)
    ym = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(ym)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= TINY:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def log_ratio(a, fix=0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    fix = int(fix) % n

    pos = a > 1e-200
    if pos.any():
        logs = np.log(a[pos])
        ref = float(np.exp(np.mean(logs)))
    else:
        ref = 1.0

    y = np.log(a / max(ref, TINY))
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-MAXY, posinf=MAXY, neginf=-MAXY)
    y = y - y[fix]
    y = np.clip(y, -MAXY, MAXY)
    y[fix] = 0.0
    return y


def softmax_weights(d, beta):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(d.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if dmax <= 1e-300 or beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    z = float(beta) * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= TINY:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    return e / s


def top_weights(d, k):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(d.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, L)))
    if k >= L:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    idx = np.argpartition(d, -k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def gap_weights(d, M, gap_frac, top_k, max_count, power=1.0):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(d.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    top_k = int(max(1, min(L, top_k)))
    max_count = int(max(1, min(L, max_count)))
    gap = float(M) * float(gap_frac)

    if gap <= 1e-300:
        return top_weights(d, top_k)

    thresh = float(M) - gap
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = d >= thresh
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))

    if cnt < top_k:
        kc = top_k
        if kc >= L:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        idx = np.argpartition(d, -kc)[-kc:]
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        vals = d[mask]
        excess = vals - thresh
        maxex = float(np.max(excess))
        if maxex <= 1e-300 or power <= 0.0:
            w[mask] = 1.0
        else:
            w[mask] = np.power(excess, float(power))

        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(w))
        s = float(w.sum())
        if cnt > max_count or s <= 1e-300:
            kc = max_count
            if kc >= L:
                return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
            idx = np.argpartition(d, -kc)[-kc:]
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            vals = d[idx]
            vmin = float(np.min(vals))
            excess = vals - vmin
            maxex = float(np.max(excess))
            if maxex <= 1e-300 or power <= 0.0:
                w[idx] = 1.0
            else:
                w[idx] = np.power(excess, float(power))
            s = float(w.sum())

    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return top_weights(d, max_count)
    return w / s


def excess_weights(d, m):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(d.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(max(1, min(m, L)))
    if m >= L:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    idx = np.argpartition(d, -m)[-m:]
    mt = float(np.mean(d[idx]))
    excess = np.maximum(d - mt, 0.0)
    s = float(excess.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return top_weights(d, m)
    return excess / s


def best_multiplicative(a, base_log, h, etas, n, start_sc):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if h.size != n:
        return a, start_sc

    hmean = float(np.mean(h))
    h = h - hmean
    hmaxp = float(np.max(h))
    hmaxn = float(-np.min(h))
    scale = max(hmaxp, hmaxn)
    if scale < 1e-16:
        return a, start_sc
    h = h / scale

    best_a = a
    best_sc = start_sc
    for eta in etas:
        eta = float(eta)
        if not np.isfinite(eta) or eta <= 0.0:
            continue
        if eta > 8.0:
            eta = 8.0
        logn = base_log - eta * h
        lmax = float(np.max(logn))
        cand = np.exp(logn - lmax)
        cand = normalize_positive(cand)
        sc = exact_score(cand)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand
    return best_a, best_sc


def smooth_noise(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    maxk = max(3, min(n, 41))
    k = int(rng.integers(3, maxk + 1))
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    if k > n:
        k = n if (n % 2 == 1) else max(1, n - 1)
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
    if k < 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = k // 2
    sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
    kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    kern /= kern.sum()
    noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, mode="same")
    noise -= float(noise.mean())
    return noise


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)
    strength = float(strength)
    if strength <= 0.0:
        return a

    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 8))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if mode == 0:
        noise = smooth_noise(n, rng)
        q = a * np.exp(strength * 0.25 * noise)
    elif mode == 1:
        q = a * np.exp(strength * 0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        f = float(rng.integers(1, 7))
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x))
    elif mode == 4:
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    elif mode == 5:
        q = a * (1.0 - strength * 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    elif mode == 6:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.4))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (0.5 * a + 0.5 * a[::-1])
    else:
        q = a * rng.uniform(0.8, 1.2, size=n)

    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize_positive(q)


def mirror_descent_optimize(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", rng=None, eta_initial=1.0):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    if verbose:
        print("[{} len={}] start score={:.10f}".format(tag, n, best_sc))

    eta = float(eta_initial)
    gap = 0.05
    topk = 20
    max_count = max(20, int(0.02 * n))
    stagnant = 0
    perturbs = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 1.5:
        conv = fast_self_conv(a)
        d = conv * float(n)
        dmax = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
        sc = 2.0 * dmax

        if not np.isfinite(best_sc) or sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if it % 16 == 0:
            sym = normalize_positive(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))
            scs = exact_score(sym)
            if scs < sc - 1e-14:
                a = sym
                sc = scs
            if scs < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = scs
                best_a = sym.copy()

        base_log = np.log(np.maximum(a, TINY))
        base_log -= float(np.max(base_log))

        Xrev, Nh = rfft_reversed(a)

        orig_sc = sc
        dir_a = a
        dir_sc = orig_sc

        etas = (max(0.02, 0.4 * eta), float(eta), min(6.0, 2.0 * eta))
        active_etas = (max(0.02, 0.3 * eta), float(eta), min(6.0, 1.8 * eta))

        for beta in (50.0, 400.0, 3000.0):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.5:
                break
            w = softmax_weights(d, beta)
            h = hankel_matvec_precomp(w, Xrev, Nh, n)
            cand_a, cand_sc = best_multiplicative(a, base_log, h, etas, n, orig_sc)
            if cand_sc < dir_sc - 1e-16:
                dir_a = cand_a
                dir_sc = cand_sc

        w = gap_weights(d, dmax, gap, max(5, topk), max_count, 1.0)
        if time.monotonic() <= float(deadline) - 1.5:
            h = hankel_matvec_precomp(w, Xrev, Nh, n)
            cand_a, cand_sc = best_multiplicative(a, base_log, h, active_etas, n, orig_sc)
            if cand_sc < dir_sc - 1e-16:
                dir_a = cand_a
                dir_sc = cand_sc

        w = top_weights(d, max(3, topk // 2))
        if time.monotonic() <= float(deadline) - 1.5:
            h = hankel_matvec_precomp(w, Xrev, Nh, n)
            cand_a, cand_sc = best_multiplicative(a, base_log, h, active_etas, n, orig_sc)
            if cand_sc < dir_sc - 1e-16:
                dir_a = cand_a
                dir_sc = cand_sc

        m_excess = max(20, min(200, int(0.02 * n)))
        w = excess_weights(d, m_excess)
        if time.monotonic() <= float(deadline) - 1.5:
            h = hankel_matvec_precomp(w, Xrev, Nh, n)
            cand_a, cand_sc = best_multiplicative(a, base_log, h, active_etas, n, orig_sc)
            if cand_sc < dir_sc - 1e-16:
                dir_a = cand_a
                dir_sc = cand_sc

        a = dir_a
        cur_sc = dir_sc

        if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if cur_sc < orig_sc - 1e-15:
            stagnant = 0
            if eta < 4.0:
                eta = min(4.0, eta * 1.03)
            if gap > 0.02:
                gap = max(0.02, gap * 0.98)
            if topk > 8:
                topk = max(8, int(topk * 0.97))
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.85)
            gap = min(0.30, gap * 1.15)
            topk = min(120, int(topk * 1.15) + 1)

            if stagnant >= 80:
                if perturbs < 5:
                    a = perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.05 + 0.05 * perturbs)
                    perturbs += 1
                    stagnant = 0
                    eta = 0.8
                    gap = 0.05
                    topk = 20
                    if verbose:
                        print("[{} i{}] perturb {} score_now={:.10f}".format(tag, it, perturbs, exact_score(a)))
                else:
                    break

        if verbose and it % 50 == 0:
            print("[{} i{}] cur={:.10f} best={:.10f} eta={:.3f} gap={:.3f} topk={}".format(
                tag, it, cur_sc, best_sc, eta, gap, topk))

        it += 1

    if verbose:
        print("[{}] done best={:.10f}".format(tag, best_sc))

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def lbfgs_softmax_fun(y, beta, fix=0):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(y.size)
    fix = int(fix) % max(1, n)

    if n == 0:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax_stable(y)

    conv = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = conv * float(n)
    d = np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dmax = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0

    if dmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= TINY:
        w = np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
        lse = dmax
    else:
        w = e / s
        lse = dmax + math.log(s) / beta

    val = 2.0 * lse

    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    grad_a = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    sdot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_y = a * (grad_a - sdot * a)
    grad_y = np.nan_to_num(grad_y, nan=0.0, posinf=1e8, neginf=-1e8)
    if grad_y.size > fix:
        grad_y[fix] = 0.0

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e12
        grad_y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(val), grad_y


def lbfgs_polish(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", rng=None):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 3:
        return exact_score(a), a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    if verbose:
        print("[{} lbfgs start] score={:.10f}".format(tag, best_sc))

    fix = int(np.argmin(np.abs(a - float(np.median(a)))))
    fix = fix % n

    bounds = [(-MAXY, MAXY)] * n
    bounds[fix] = (0.0, 0.0)

    if n <= 12000:
        mf_base = 180
    elif n <= 25000:
        mf_base = 110
    else:
        mf_base = 65

    per_eval = 0.05 if n <= 12000 else (0.10 if n <= 25000 else 0.18)

    for beta in (50.0, 150.0, 400.0, 1000.0, 3000.0, 8000.0, 15000.0):
        rem = float(deadline) - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break

        mf = min(mf_base, max(20, int(rem * 0.55 / max(0.02, per_eval))))
        y0 = log_ratio(best_a, fix)

        opts = {
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-5,
            "maxiter": int(mf),
            "maxfun": int(mf),
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxcor": 20,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbfgs_softmax_fun,
                y0,
                args=(float(beta), fix),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options=opts,
            )
            x1 = res.x
        except Exception:
            x1 = y0

        cand = softmax_stable(x1)
        cand = normalize_positive(cand)
        sc = exact_score(cand)

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            if verbose:
                print("[{} lbfgs beta={}] score={:.10f}".format(tag, beta, best_sc))

    if verbose:
        print("[{} lbfgs done] score={:.10f}".format(tag, best_sc))

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def optimize_level(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag="", rng=None):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(0.0, float(deadline) - t0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    if total < 15.0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    if verbose:
        print("== {} len={} total={:.1f}s initial={:.10f}".format(tag, n, total, best_sc))

    if total > 600.0:
        d1 = t0 + 0.62 * total
        d2 = t0 + 0.84 * total
    else:
        d1 = t0 + 0.55 * total
        d2 = t0 + 0.80 * total

    sc, a1 = mirror_descent_optimize(a, d1, verbose=verbose, tag=tag + "/m1", rng=rng)
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a1.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < float(d2) - 10.0:
        sc, a2 = lbfgs_polish(best_a, d2, verbose=verbose, tag=tag + "/l", rng=rng)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 10.0:
        sc, a3 = mirror_descent_optimize(best_a, deadline, verbose=verbose,
                                         tag=tag + "/m2", rng=rng, eta_initial=0.6)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a3.copy()

    if verbose:
        print("== {} len={} finished best={:.10f}".format(tag, n, best_sc))

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def choose_best_init(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cands = []

    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        cands.append(1.0 + float(alpha) * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        cands.append(1.0 + float(alpha) * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)), float(p)) + 0.01)

    for rate in (1.0, 3.0, 8.0):
        cands.append(np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + 0.01)

    N = next_fft_len(n)
    for _ in range(4):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(N) * float(N)
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 4.0)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, N)[:n]
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        cands.append(1.0 + 0.6 * noise)

    for _ in range(2):
        cands.append(0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(n))

    best_a = normalize_positive(cands[0])
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)

    for c in cands[1:]:
        arr = normalize_positive(c)
        sc = exact_score(arr)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = arr

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = t_start + total

    reserve = min(180.0, max(60.0, 0.08 * total))
    search_deadline = t_start + total - reserve
    if search_deadline < t_start + 30.0:
        search_deadline = t_start + total - 10.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 32 and float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose increasing target lengths.
    if parent is not None:
        L = int(parent.size)
        base_len = L
        if base_len < 10240:
            base_len = 10240
        elif base_len <= 12288:
            base_len = 10240 if base_len < 10240 else base_len
        elif base_len > 43680:
            base_len = 43680
        levels = [base_len]
        for tgt in (20480, 32768, 43680):
            if tgt > levels[-1]:
                levels.append(tgt)
    else:
        levels = [10240, 20480, 32768, 43680]

    # Initial sequence.
    if parent is not None:
        current = normalize_positive(parent)
        if current.size != levels[0]:
            current = resample_sequence(current, levels[0])
        current = normalize_positive(current)
        best_score = exact_score(current)
        best_seq = current.copy()
        print("Parent len={} initial score={:.10f}".format(current.size, best_score))
    else:
        current = None
        best_score = np.inf
        best_seq = None
        best_score, best_seq = choose_best_init(levels[0], rng)
        best_score = float(best_score)
        print("No parent. Initial n={} score={:.10f}".format(levels[0], best_score))

    total_search = max(30.0, float(search_deadline) - t_start)

    if len(levels) == 1:
        weights = [1.0]
    elif len(levels) == 2:
        weights = [0.25, 0.75]
        if parent is None:
            weights = [0.45, 0.55]
    elif len(levels) == 3:
        weights = [0.10, 0.35, 0.55]
        if parent is None:
            weights = [0.25, 0.30, 0.45]
    else:
        weights = [0.05, 0.20, 0.25, 0.50]
        if parent is None:
            weights = [0.18, 0.20, 0.24, 0.38]

    wsum = float(sum(weights))

    acc = 0.0
    for idx, nlev in enumerate(levels):
        acc += weights[idx]
        planned_dead = t_start + acc * total_search
        planned_dead = max(planned_dead, time.monotonic() + 20.0)
        planned_dead = min(planned_dead, float(search_deadline))

        need_run = (idx == 0) or (nlev > int(best_seq.size))
        if not need_run:
            continue
        if planned_dead <= time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            continue

        if idx == 0 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == nlev:
            a0 = best_seq.copy()
        else:
            a0 = resample_sequence(best_seq, nlev)
            a0 = normalize_positive(a0)

        sc, seq = optimize_level(a0, planned_dead, verbose=True,
                                 tag="L{}-n{}".format(idx, nlev), rng=rng)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("New best at level n={}: {:.10f}".format(nlev, best_score))

    # Final short polish at the best length found.
    final_dead = min(hard_deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
    if final_dead > time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        sc, seq = mirror_descent_optimize(best_seq, final_dead, verbose=False,
                                          tag="final", rng=rng, eta_initial=0.4)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("Final polish improved to {:.10f}".format(best_score))

    out = finalize_sequence(best_seq)
    print("DONE best internal={:.10f} output n={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_score, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start))
    return out
```
